## Australia and New Zealand Tariff Preference Maintenance Order {#australia-and-new-zealand-tariff-preference-maintenance-order .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/95-465
:::

[CUSTOMS TARIFF](/eng/acts/C-54.011)

Registration 1995-09-26

Order Respecting Australia and New Zealand Rates of Customs Duty

P.C. 1995-1609 1995-09-26
::::

::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Whereas certain concessions relating to Canadian goods have been granted
to Canada by Australia and New Zealand;

Therefore, His Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the
recommendation of the Minister of Finance, pursuant to section
12.1[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#footnote1star_e){#footnote1star_e-ID0EGDAA} and
subsection 62(1)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*\*^](#footnote2star_e){#footnote2star_e-ID0EEDAA} of
the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*\*\*^](#footnote3star_e){#footnote3star_e-ID0EBDAA},
is pleased hereby to make the annexed Order respecting Australia and New
Zealand rates of customs duty.

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#footnote1star_e-ID0EGDAA){#footnote1star_e}S.C.
  1993, c. 25, s. 1
  :::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^\*\*^](#footnote2star_e-ID0EEDAA){#footnote2star_e}S.C.
  1994, c. 47, s. 86
  :::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^\*\*\*^](#footnote3star_e-ID0EBDAA){#footnote3star_e}R.S., c.
  41 (3rd Supp.)
  :::
:::

## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-976009 .Part}

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Order may be cited as
the [Australia and New Zealand Tariff Preference Maintenance
Order](/eng/regulations/SOR-95-465).

## [Extension of Benefits]{.HTitleText1} {#h-976011 .Part}

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** For each tariff item No. or
Code set out in column I of Parts I to III of the schedule, the rates of
customs duty set out in column II of that tariff item No. or Code are
extended to the goods of that tariff item No. or Code imported from
Australia or New Zealand on and after the dates set out in column II of
that tariff item No. or Code.

## [Customs Tariff Amendments]{.HTitleText1} {#h-976013 .Part}

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** Schedule I to the [Customs
Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) is amended by adding, for each tariff item
No. set out in column I of Part I of the schedule, under the heading
"Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff", the rates of customs duty set out in
column II of that tariff item No.

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** Schedule I to the [Customs
Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) is amended by replacing, for each tariff
item No. set out in column I of Part II of the schedule, the rate of
customs duty in respect of Australia or New Zealand, as the case may be,
set out under the heading "Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff" with the rates
of customs duty set out in column II of that tariff item No.

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** Schedule II to the [Customs
Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) is amended by adding, after the British
Preferential Tariff rates of duty under the heading
"Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff", for each Code set out in column I of Part
III of the schedule, the rates of customs duty set out in column II of
that Code.
